# the Lady of the Lake clergy organize a public rite to focus religious authority.
country_event = {
	id = war_culture_and_religion_events.1
	title = war_culture_and_religion_events.1.t
	desc = war_culture_and_religion_events.1.d
	picture = BRETONNIA_eventPicture
	# Event Weighing Value: +1

	fire_only_once = no
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		religion = lady_of_the_lake
	}

	mean_time_to_happen = {
		days = 1
	}

# Fund rite.
	option = {
		ai_chance = { factor = 50 }
		name = war_culture_and_religion_events.1.a
		# Weighing Value: +1

		add_years_of_income = -0.5
		add_country_modifier = {
			name = war_religious_fervor
			duration = 5475
		}
		# Balancing adjustment to match event Weighing Value: +1
		# Adjustment Weighing Value: -1 (-50 MIL power)
		add_mil_power = -50

	}

# Appoint wardens.
	option = {
		ai_chance = { factor = 50 }
		name = war_culture_and_religion_events.1.b
		# Weighing Value: +1

		add_dip_power = -50
		add_country_modifier = {
			name = war_better_religious_unity
			duration = 5475
		}
	}

# Keep balance.
	option = {
		ai_chance = { factor = 50 }
		name = war_culture_and_religion_events.1.c
		# Weighing Value: +1

		add_adm_power = 50
	}
}

		}

		add_devotion = 10

		add_meritocracy = 10
		add_yearly_manpower = 1
		# Balancing adjustment to match event Weighing Value: 0
		# Adjustment Weighing Value: -1 (-1 innovativeness)
		change_innovativeness = -1

	}

		ai_chance = { factor = 50 }

		ai_chance = { factor = 50 }
		name = war_culture_and_religion_events.225.husk
		highlight = yes
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				has_ruler = "Husk-of-Nagash"
				has_ruler = "Nagash"
			}
		}
		# Weighing Value: +1

		add_years_of_income = 0.5
	}
